157	  def destroy(sandbox:, archive: false)
158	    begin
159	      TerminalManager.new.close(sandbox: sandbox)
160	    rescue TerminalManager::Error, Docker::Error::DockerError
161	      # best-effort terminal cleanup
162	    end
163	
164	    begin
165	      VncManager.new.close(sandbox: sandbox)
166	    rescue VncManager::Error, Docker::Error::DockerError
167	      # best-effort VNC cleanup
168	    end
169	
170	    RouteManager.new.remove_all_routes(sandbox: sandbox) if sandbox.routed?
171	
172	    if sandbox.tailscale?
173	      TailscaleManager.new.disconnect_sandbox(sandbox: sandbox)
174	    end
175	
176	    NetworkManager.new.disconnect_sandbox(sandbox: sandbox)
177	
178	    if sandbox.container_id.present?
179	      begin
180	        container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
181	        container.stop(t: 5) rescue nil
182	        container.delete(force: true)
183	      rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
184	        # Container already gone
185	      end
186	    end
187	
188	    if archive
189	      # Soft-delete: keep volume on disk, mark as archived.
190	      # Rename to free the original name for reuse.
191	      archived_name = "#{Time.current.strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}-#{sandbox.name}"
192	      sandbox.update!(status: "archived", container_id: nil, archived_at: Time.current, name: archived_name)
193	    else
194	      sandbox.update!(status: "destroyed", container_id: nil)
195	    end
196	
197	    # Remove per-user network if this was the last active sandbox
198	    begin
199	      NetworkManager.new.cleanup_user_network(sandbox.user)
200	    rescue NetworkManager::Error => e
201	      Rails.logger.warn("SandboxManager#destroy: network cleanup failed for #{sandbox.user.name}: #{e.message}")
202	    end
203	  end
204	
205	  # Restore an archived sandbox: recreate the container from the existing volume.
206	  # The container is started immediately; status is set to "running".
207	  def restore_from_archive(sandbox:)
208	    raise Error, "Sandbox is not archived" unless sandbox.status == "archived"
209	
210	    user = sandbox.user
211	
212	    ensure_image(sandbox.image)
213	    ensure_mount_dirs(user, sandbox)
214	
215	    info = fetch_image_info(sandbox.image)
216	    sandbox.update!(image_id: info[:image_id], image_built_at: info[:image_built_at], image_version: info[:image_version])
217	
218	    # Strip the archival timestamp prefix (e.g. "20260307123456-mybox" → "mybox")
219	    # BEFORE status transitions out of "archived", otherwise the name format
220	    # validator runs against the prefixed name and fails.
221	    original_name = sandbox.name.sub(/\A\d{14}-/, "")
222	    sandbox.update!(archived_at: nil, name: original_name)
223	
224	    create_container_and_start(sandbox: sandbox, user: user)
225	
226	    if sandbox.tailscale? && user.tailscale_enabled?
227	      TailscaleManager.new.connect_sandbox(sandbox: sandbox)
228	    end
229	
230	    sandbox
231	  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
232	    raise Error, "Failed to restore archived sandbox: #{e.message}"
233	  end
234	
235	  def start(sandbox:)
236	    raise Error, "Sandbox is destroyed" if sandbox.status == "destroyed"
237	    return sandbox if sandbox.status == "running"
238	
239	    user = sandbox.user
240	
241	    # Try to restart the existing container to preserve Sysbox UID mapping.
242	    # Recreating the container assigns a new UID offset, which causes
243	    # bind-mounted files to appear owned by the wrong user.
244	    if sandbox.container_id.present?
245	      restarted = try_restart_existing(sandbox)
246	      if restarted

> AGENT

Root cause identified: sandbox 5 has `job_status: "destroying"` stuck in the DB from a past attempt that didn't clear it (worker killed before `finish_job`/`fail_job` could run). The controller's `job_in_progress?` check has no staleness threshold, so it blocks forever.

Let me fix both the specific sandbox and the underlying code:

50	  # Whether SSH logins should auto-attach to a tmux session. Per-sandbox
51	  # override (nullable) wins; otherwise falls back to the user's default.
52	  def effective_ssh_start_tmux?
53	    return ssh_start_tmux unless ssh_start_tmux.nil?
54	    user.default_ssh_start_tmux?
55	  end
56	
57	  # Job lifecycle management
58	  def job_in_progress?
59	    job_status.present?
60	  end
61	
62	  def job_failed?
63	    job_error.present?
64	  end
65	
66	  def start_job(status)
67	    update!(job_status: status, job_started_at: Time.current, job_error: nil)
68	  end
69	
70	  def finish_job
71	    update!(job_status: nil, job_started_at: nil)
72	  end
73	
74	  def fail_job(error_message)
75	    # Bypass validations — this runs inside a job's rescue, and if the
76	    # underlying failure was itself a validation error, update! would
77	    # re-raise and leave job_status stuck forever. update_columns also
78	    # skips callbacks, which is the right behavior here (no broadcast
79	    # for "we failed to record a failure").
80	    update_columns(
81	      job_status: nil,
82	      job_error: error_message,
83	      job_started_at: nil,
84	      updated_at: Time.current
85	    )
86	    broadcast_replace_to_dashboard
87	  end
88	
89	  private

  "new_string": "  # Job lifecycle management\n  # A job is only considered \"in progress\" while still within this window.\n  # Past this, the claim is treated as stale — the worker almost certainly\n  # crashed or was killed (SIGKILL, OOM, container restart, deploy) before\n  # it could call finish_job / fail_job, so we let the next click go\n  # through instead of blocking the sandbox forever.\n  JOB_STALE_AFTER = 5.minutes\n\n  def job_in_progress?\n    return false if job_status.blank?\n    return true if job_started_at.nil?\n    job_started_at > JOB_STALE_AFTER.ago\n  end"
}

1	class ContainerSyncJob < ApplicationJob
2	  queue_as :default
3	
4	  def perform
5	    Sandbox.active.where.not(container_id: nil).find_each do |sandbox|
6	      sync_sandbox(sandbox)
7	    end
8	
9	    User.where(tailscale_state: [ "enabled", "pending" ]).find_each do |user|
10	      sync_tailscale_sidecar(user)
11	    end
12	
13	    User.where(tailscale_state: "disabled").find_each do |user|
14	      restore_tailscale_from_saved_key(user)
15	    end
16	
17	    # Reconcile per-user network membership for running sandboxes
18	    sync_user_networks
19	
20	    begin
21	      RouteManager.new.sync_all_configs
22	    rescue => e
23	      Rails.logger.error("ContainerSyncJob: route sync failed: #{e.message}")
24	    end
25	
26	    begin
27	      TerminalManager.new.cleanup_orphaned
28	    rescue => e
29	      Rails.logger.error("ContainerSyncJob: terminal cleanup failed: #{e.message}")
30	    end
31	
32	    begin
33	      VncManager.new.cleanup_orphaned
34	    rescue => e
35	      Rails.logger.error("ContainerSyncJob: VNC cleanup failed: #{e.message}")
36	    end
37	  end
38	
39	  private
40	
41	  def sync_sandbox(sandbox)
42	    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
43	    state = container.json["State"] || {}
44	    actual_status = if state["Restarting"]
45	      "stopped"
46	    elsif state["Running"]
47	      "running"
48	    else
49	      "stopped"
50	    end
51	
52	    if sandbox.status != actual_status
53	      if actual_status == "stopped"
54	        begin
55	          TerminalManager.new.close(sandbox: sandbox)
56	        rescue TerminalManager::Error, Docker::Error::DockerError
57	          # best-effort
58	        end
59	        begin
60	          VncManager.new.close(sandbox: sandbox)
61	        rescue VncManager::Error, Docker::Error::DockerError
62	          # best-effort
63	        end
64	      end
65	      sandbox.update!(status: actual_status)
66	      Rails.logger.info("ContainerSyncJob: #{sandbox.full_name} status corrected to #{actual_status}")
67	    end
68	
69	    # Backfill image tracking for sandboxes created before the migration
70	    if sandbox.image_id.nil? && sandbox.status == "running"
71	      info = SandboxManager.new.send(:fetch_image_info, sandbox.image)
72	      sandbox.update!(image_id: info[:image_id], image_built_at: info[:image_built_at]) if info[:image_id]
73	    end
74	  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
75	    begin
76	      TerminalManager.new.close(sandbox: sandbox)
77	    rescue TerminalManager::Error, Docker::Error::DockerError
78	      # best-effort
79	    end
80	    begin
81	      VncManager.new.close(sandbox: sandbox)
82	    rescue VncManager::Error, Docker::Error::DockerError
83	      # best-effort
84	    end
85	    sandbox.update!(status: "destroyed", container_id: nil)
86	    Rails.logger.warn("ContainerSyncJob: #{sandbox.full_name} container gone, marked destroyed")
87	  end
88	
89	  def restore_tailscale_from_saved_key(user)
90	    tm = TailscaleManager.new
91	    auth_key = File.read(tm.auth_key_path(user)).strip rescue nil
92	    if auth_key.present?
93	      tm.enable(user: user, auth_key: auth_key)
94	      Rails.logger.info("ContainerSyncJob: restored Tailscale for #{user.name} from saved auth key")
95	      return
96	    end
97	
98	    # Fallback: restore from persisted tailscaled.state (interactive-login survivors).
99	    # We check if the tailscale *directory* exists (readable via parent dir's 755 perms)
100	    # rather than the state file inside it (which is in a root-owned drwx------ dir).
101	    state_dir = File.join(TailscaleManager::DATA_DIR, "users", user.name, "tailscale")
102	    return unless File.directory?(state_dir)
103	
104	    tm.restore_from_state(user: user)
105	    Rails.logger.info("ContainerSyncJob: restored Tailscale for #{user.name} from saved state")
106	  rescue TailscaleManager::Error => e
107	    Rails.logger.warn("ContainerSyncJob: Tailscale restore for #{user.name} failed: #{e.message}")
108	  rescue => e
109	    Rails.logger.error("ContainerSyncJob: Tailscale restore for #{user.name} unexpected error: #{e.message}")
110	  end
111	
112	  def sync_user_networks
113	    nm = NetworkManager.new
114	
115	    # Ensure all running sandboxes are connected to their user's per-user network
116	    Sandbox.running.where.not(container_id: nil).find_each do |sandbox|
117	      nm.ensure_sandbox_connected(sandbox: sandbox)
118	    rescue => e
119	      Rails.logger.error("ContainerSyncJob: user network sync failed for #{sandbox.full_name}: #{e.message}")
120	    end
121	
122	    # Clean up orphaned user networks (user has no active sandboxes)
123	    User.where.not(network_name: nil).find_each do |user|
124	      nm.cleanup_user_network(user) unless user.sandboxes.active.exists?
125	    rescue => e
126	      Rails.logger.error("ContainerSyncJob: user network cleanup failed for #{user.name}: #{e.message}")
127	    end
128	  rescue => e
129	    Rails.logger.error("ContainerSyncJob: sync_user_networks failed: #{e.message}")
130	  end
131	
132	  def sync_tailscale_sidecar(user)
133	    return if user.tailscale_container_id.blank?
134	
135	    Docker::Container.get(user.tailscale_container_id)
136	  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
137	    was_enabled = user.tailscale_enabled?
138	    user.update!(tailscale_state: "disabled", tailscale_container_id: nil, tailscale_network: nil)
139	    user.sandboxes.active.where(tailscale: true).update_all(tailscale: false)
140	    Rails.logger.warn("ContainerSyncJob: Tailscale sidecar for #{user.name} gone, marked disabled")
141	
142	    # If user had a live sidecar (not just pending login), try to restore from
143	    # persisted tailscaled.state so they reconnect automatically after reinstall.
144	    # The tailscale dir is root-owned so we can't check File.exist? — just attempt it.
145	    return unless was_enabled
146	
147	    begin
148	      TailscaleManager.new.restore_from_state(user: user.reload)
149	      Rails.logger.info("ContainerSyncJob: Tailscale sidecar for #{user.name} restored from saved state")
150	    rescue TailscaleManager::Error => e
151	      Rails.logger.warn("ContainerSyncJob: Tailscale state restore for #{user.name} failed: #{e.message}")
152	    rescue => e
153	      Rails.logger.error("ContainerSyncJob: Tailscale state restore for #{user.name} unexpected error: #{e.message}")
154	    end
155	  end
156	end

235	  def start(sandbox:)
236	    raise Error, "Sandbox is destroyed" if sandbox.status == "destroyed"
237	    return sandbox if sandbox.status == "running"
238	
239	    user = sandbox.user
240	
241	    # Try to restart the existing container to preserve Sysbox UID mapping.
242	    # Recreating the container assigns a new UID offset, which causes
243	    # bind-mounted files to appear owned by the wrong user.
244	    if sandbox.container_id.present?
245	      restarted = try_restart_existing(sandbox)
246	      if restarted
247	        TailscaleManager.new.connect_sandbox(sandbox: sandbox) if sandbox.tailscale? && user.tailscale_enabled?
248	        RouteManager.new.reconnect_routes(sandbox: sandbox) if sandbox.routed?
249	        return sandbox
250	      end
251	    end
252	
253	    # Container gone or restart failed — recreate from scratch.
254	    ensure_mount_dirs(user, sandbox)
255	
256	    # Refresh image info in case the image was updated since sandbox creation
257	    info = fetch_image_info(sandbox.image)
258	    sandbox.update!(image_id: info[:image_id], image_built_at: info[:image_built_at], image_version: info[:image_version])
259	
260	    create_container_and_start(sandbox: sandbox, user: user)
261	
262	    TailscaleManager.new.connect_sandbox(sandbox: sandbox) if sandbox.tailscale? && user.tailscale_enabled?
263	    RouteManager.new.reconnect_routes(sandbox: sandbox) if sandbox.routed?
264	
265	    sandbox
266	  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
267	    sandbox.update!(status: "destroyed", container_id: nil)
268	    raise Error, "Container not found — sandbox must be recreated"
269	  end
270	
271	  def rebuild(sandbox:)
272	    raise Error, "Sandbox is destroyed" if sandbox.status == "destroyed"
273	
274	    user = sandbox.user
275	
276	    # Destroy the existing container so we get a fresh one with the latest image.
277	    if sandbox.container_id.present?
278	      begin
279	        old = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
280	        old.stop(t: 5) rescue nil
281	        old.delete(force: true)
282	      rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
283	        # already gone
284	      end
285	    end
286	
287	    ensure_mount_dirs(user, sandbox)
288	
289	    info = fetch_image_info(sandbox.image)
290	    sandbox.update!(image_id: info[:image_id], image_built_at: info[:image_built_at], image_version: info[:image_version])
291	
292	    create_container_and_start(sandbox: sandbox, user: user)
293	
294	    TailscaleManager.new.connect_sandbox(sandbox: sandbox) if sandbox.tailscale? && user.tailscale_enabled?
295	    RouteManager.new.reconnect_routes(sandbox: sandbox) if sandbox.routed?
296	
297	    sandbox
298	  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
299	    sandbox.update!(status: "destroyed", container_id: nil)
300	    raise Error, "Container not found — sandbox must be recreated"
301	  end
302	
303	  def stop(sandbox:)
304	    return sandbox if sandbox.status == "stopped"
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/persisted/Projects/Sandcastle/images/sandbox/entrypoint.sh",
  "old_string": "# Generate SSH host keys if missing\nssh-keygen -A",
  "new_string": "# Allow SSH clients to forward NO_TMUX so users can bypass the tmux\n# auto-attach for a single connection (e.g. `ssh -o SetEnv=NO_TMUX=1 ...`).\n# Idempotent: the file is regenerated on every container start.\nif [ -d /etc/ssh/sshd_config.d ] && ! grep -qs '^AcceptEnv NO_TMUX' /etc/ssh/sshd_config.d/*.conf 2>/dev/null; then\n    echo \"AcceptEnv NO_TMUX\" >> /etc/ssh/sshd_config.d/20-sandcastle-env.conf\nfi\n\n# Generate SSH host keys if missing\nssh-keygen -A"
}
```

{

  "old_string": "# >>> sandcastle ssh tmux auto-attach >>>\n# Auto-attach to a shared tmux session for interactive SSH logins.\n# Toggle via the sandbox's \"Start tmux on SSH\" setting.\nif [ -z \"$TMUX\" ] && [ -n \"$SSH_CONNECTION\" ] && [[ $- == *i* ]] && command -v tmux >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    exec tmux new-session -A -s main\nfi\n# <<< sandcastle ssh tmux auto-attach <<<",
  "new_string": "# >>> sandcastle ssh tmux auto-attach >>>\n# Auto-attach to a shared tmux session for interactive SSH logins.\n# Toggle per-sandbox via the \"Start tmux on SSH\" setting.\n# Bypass for a single connection with: ssh -o SetEnv=NO_TMUX=1 ...\nif [ -z \"$TMUX\" ] && [ -z \"$NO_TMUX\" ] && [ -n \"$SSH_CONNECTION\" ] && [[ $- == *i* ]] && command -v tmux >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    exec tmux new-session -A -s main\nfi\n# <<< sandcastle ssh tmux auto-attach <<<"
}

  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/persisted/Projects/Sandcastle/app/controllers/sandboxes_controller.rb",
  "old_string": "  def update\n    if @sandbox.update(params.require(:sandbox).permit(:name, :ssh_start_tmux))\n      notice = params[:sandbox][:ssh_start_tmux].nil? ? \"Sandbox renamed to #{@sandbox.name}.\" : \"Sandbox settings updated. Takes effect on next sandbox start.\"\n      redirect_to @sandbox, notice: notice\n    else",
  "new_string": "  def update\n    sandbox_params = params.require(:sandbox).permit(\n      :name, :ssh_start_tmux, :mount_home, :docker_enabled,\n      :vnc_enabled, :vnc_geometry, :vnc_depth, :smb_enabled\n    )\n\n    # Only name may be edited while the sandbox is running; everything else\n    # needs a rebuild/restart to take effect, so we reject settings edits to\n    # avoid silently-stored-but-ignored values.\n    if @sandbox.status == \"running\" && sandbox_params.except(:name).any?\n      redirect_to @sandbox, alert: \"Stop the sandbox before editing settings.\" and return\n    end\n\n    if @sandbox.update(sandbox_params)\n      notice = sandbox_params.keys == [\"name\"] ? \"Sandbox renamed to #{@sandbox.name}.\" : \"Sandbox settings updated. Changes apply on next rebuild/start.\"\n      redirect_to @sandbox, notice: notice\n    else"
}

29	      redirect_to @sandbox, alert: "Stop the sandbox before editing settings." and return
30	    end
31	
32	    if @sandbox.update(sandbox_params)
33	      notice = sandbox_params.keys == ["name"] ? "Sandbox renamed to #{@sandbox.name}." : "Sandbox settings updated. Changes apply on next rebuild/start."
34	      redirect_to @sandbox, notice: notice
35	    else
36	      @sandbox_snapshots = SandboxManager.new.list_snapshots(user: Current.user)
37	                             .select { |s| s[:source_sandbox] == @sandbox.name }
38	      @routes = @sandbox.routes.order(:created_at)
39	      @btrfs = BtrfsHelper.btrfs?
40	      flash.now[:alert] = @sandbox.errors.full_messages.join(", ")
41	      render :show, status: :unprocessable_entity
42	    end
43	  end
```

{
  "command": "git add app/models/sandbox.rb && git commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nfix(sandbox): treat job_in_progress as stale after 5 minutes\n\nA sandbox on production had been stuck in job_status='destroying' for\n24 days because a past destroy job exited without calling finish_job or\nfail_job — most likely the worker was killed mid-run (deploy, OOM,\nSIGKILL). With no self-healing, every subsequent \"Really delete\" click\nhit the job_in_progress? guard and bailed out with \"Operation already in\nprogress\", with no path forward.\n\nNow job_in_progress? only reports true while job_started_at is within\na 5-minute window; stale claims are ignored so the next action proceeds\nand overwrites the claim via start_job.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Commit stale-job fix"
}

All three commits pushed and tagged **v0.8.100**:

- `ce9ff04` fix(sandbox): treat job_in_progress as stale after 5 minutes

**What was done for sandman "really delete" issue:**

Root cause: sandbox 5 had `job_status: "destroying"` stuck from 24 days ago — a past destroy job exited without calling `finish_job`/`fail_job` (worker killed mid-run), and `job_in_progress?` had no staleness check, so every retry bailed with "Operation already in progress".

Fix: `job_in_progress?` now returns false once `job_started_at` is older than 5 minutes. Live-patched on sandman, then verified by enqueuing a fresh destroy — sandbox 5 is now `status: destroyed`.

**Still need to deploy the full Rails app** to sandman to pick up the NO_TMUX + settings-edit features (those ship in the Rails image, not just the CLI). The `mise run release` you just ran produces the CLI release; let me know when you want me to do the Rails image build/deploy.

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/persisted/Projects/Sandcastle/app/services/sandbox_manager.rb",
  "old_string": "  def start(sandbox:)\n    raise Error, \"Sandbox is destroyed\" if sandbox.status == \"destroyed\"\n    return sandbox if sandbox.status == \"running\"\n\n    user = sandbox.user\n\n    # Try to restart the existing container to preserve Sysbox UID mapping.\n    # Recreating the container assigns a new UID offset, which causes\n    # bind-mounted files to appear owned by the wrong user.\n    if sandbox.container_id.present?\n      restarted = try_restart_existing(sandbox)\n      if restarted\n        TailscaleManager.new.connect_sandbox(sandbox: sandbox) if sandbox.tailscale? && user.tailscale_enabled?\n        RouteManager.new.reconnect_routes(sandbox: sandbox) if sandbox.routed?\n        return sandbox\n      end\n    end\n\n    # Container gone or restart failed — recreate from scratch.\n    ensure_mount_dirs(user, sandbox)\n\n    # Refresh image info in case the image was updated since sandbox creation\n    info = fetch_image_info(sandbox.image)\n    sandbox.update!(image_id: info[:image_id], image_built_at: info[:image_built_at], image_version: info[:image_version])\n\n    create_container_and_start(sandbox: sandbox, user: user)\n\n    TailscaleManager.new.connect_sandbox(sandbox: sandbox) if sandbox.tailscale? && user.tailscale_enabled?\n    RouteManager.new.reconnect_routes(sandbox: sandbox) if sandbox.routed?\n\n    sandbox\n  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError\n    sandbox.update!(status: \"destroyed\", container_id: nil)\n    raise Error, \"Container not found — sandbox must be recreated\"\n  end",
  "new_string": "  def start(sandbox:)\n    raise Error, \"Sandbox is destroyed\" if sandbox.status == \"destroyed\"\n    return sandbox if sandbox.status == \"running\"\n\n    user = sandbox.user\n\n    # Always recreate the container on start so the latest SSH keys and\n    # sandbox settings take effect (env vars are baked in at creation and\n    # `docker restart` would preserve the old ones). The entrypoint's\n    # chown of $HOME handles the Sysbox UID offset shift for bind mounts.\n    if sandbox.container_id.present?\n      begin\n        old = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)\n        old.stop(t: 5) rescue nil\n        old.delete(force: true)\n      rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError\n        # already gone\n      end\n    end\n\n    ensure_mount_dirs(user, sandbox)\n\n    # Refresh image info in case the image was updated since sandbox creation\n    info = fetch_image_info(sandbox.image)\n    sandbox.update!(image_id: info[:image_id], image_built_at: info[:image_built_at], image_version: info[:image_version])\n\n    create_container_and_start(sandbox: sandbox, user: user)\n\n    TailscaleManager.new.connect_sandbox(sandbox: sandbox) if sandbox.tailscale? && user.tailscale_enabled?\n    RouteManager.new.reconnect_routes(sandbox: sandbox) if sandbox.routed?\n\n    sandbox\n  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError\n    sandbox.update!(status: \"destroyed\", container_id: nil)\n    raise Error, \"Container not found — sandbox must be recreated\"\n  end"
}

1	class Sandbox < ApplicationRecord
2	  include ActionView::RecordIdentifier  # For dom_id in Turbo broadcasts
3	
4	  belongs_to :user
5	  has_many :routes, dependent: :destroy
6	  has_many :container_metrics, dependent: :delete_all
7	
8	  VNC_GEOMETRIES = %w[1280x900 1366x768 1440x900 1600x900 1920x1080 2560x1440].freeze
9	  VNC_DEPTHS = [ 8, 16, 24, 32 ].freeze
10	
11	  validates :name, presence: true,
12	    uniqueness: { scope: :user_id, conditions: -> { where.not(status: %w[destroyed archived]) } }
13	  validates :name, format: { with: /\A[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{0,62}\z/, message: "must be lowercase alphanumeric" },
14	    unless: -> { status.in?(%w[destroyed archived]) }
15	  validates :status, inclusion: { in: %w[pending running stopped destroyed archived] }
16	  validates :image, presence: true
17	  validates :vnc_geometry, inclusion: { in: VNC_GEOMETRIES }
18	  validates :vnc_depth, inclusion: { in: VNC_DEPTHS }
19	  validate :smb_prerequisites, if: -> { smb_enabled? }
20	
21	  scope :active, -> { where.not(status: %w[destroyed archived]) }
22	  scope :archived, -> { where(status: "archived") }
23	  scope :running, -> { where(status: "running") }
24	
25	  # Turbo Streams for real-time UI updates
26	  after_create_commit :broadcast_prepend_to_dashboard
27	  after_update_commit :broadcast_replace_to_dashboard
28	  after_destroy_commit :broadcast_remove_from_dashboard
29	
30	  def full_name
31	    "#{user.name}-#{name}"
32	  end
33	
34	  def connect_command
35	    "sandcastle connect #{name}"
36	  end
37	
38	  def routed?
39	    routes.loaded? ? routes.any? : routes.exists?
40	  end
41	
42	  def temp?
43	    temporary?
44	  end
45	
46	  def smb_enabled?
47	    smb_enabled
48	  end
49	
50	  # Whether SSH logins should auto-attach to a tmux session. Per-sandbox
51	  # override (nullable) wins; otherwise falls back to the user's default.
52	  def effective_ssh_start_tmux?
53	    return ssh_start_tmux unless ssh_start_tmux.nil?
54	    user.default_ssh_start_tmux?
55	  end
56	
57	  # Job lifecycle management
58	  # A job is only considered "in progress" while still within this window.
59	  # Past this, the claim is treated as stale — the worker almost certainly
60	  # crashed or was killed (SIGKILL, OOM, container restart, deploy) before
61	  # it could call finish_job / fail_job, so we let the next click go
62	  # through instead of blocking the sandbox forever.
63	  JOB_STALE_AFTER = 5.minutes
64	
65	  def job_in_progress?
66	    return false if job_status.blank?
67	    return true if job_started_at.nil?
68	    job_started_at > JOB_STALE_AFTER.ago
69	  end
70	
71	  def job_failed?
72	    job_error.present?
73	  end
74	
75	  def start_job(status)
76	    update!(job_status: status, job_started_at: Time.current, job_error: nil)
77	  end
78	
79	  def finish_job
80	    update!(job_status: nil, job_started_at: nil)
81	  end
82	
83	  def fail_job(error_message)
84	    # Bypass validations — this runs inside a job's rescue, and if the
85	    # underlying failure was itself a validation error, update! would
86	    # re-raise and leave job_status stuck forever. update_columns also
87	    # skips callbacks, which is the right behavior here (no broadcast
88	    # for "we failed to record a failure").
89	    update_columns(
90	      job_status: nil,
91	      job_error: error_message,
92	      job_started_at: nil,
93	      updated_at: Time.current
94	    )
95	    broadcast_replace_to_dashboard
96	  end
97	
98	  private
99	
100	  def smb_prerequisites
101	    errors.add(:smb_enabled, "requires Tailscale to be enabled") unless user&.tailscale_enabled?
102	    errors.add(:smb_enabled, "requires an SMB password to be set in Settings") unless user&.smb_password.present?
103	  end
104	
105	  def broadcast_prepend_to_dashboard
106	    broadcast_prepend_to(
107	      [ user, "dashboard" ],
108	      partial: "dashboard/sandbox",
109	      locals: { sandbox: self },
110	      target: "sandboxes"
111	    )
112	  rescue => e
113	    Rails.logger.warn("Sandbox#broadcast_prepend: #{e.message}")
114	  end
115	
116	  def broadcast_replace_to_dashboard
117	    prev_status = status_previously_was
118	
119	    if status == "destroyed"
120	      # Purge from wherever the row lives (active or archived list).
121	      broadcast_remove_to([ user, "dashboard" ], target: dom_id(self))
122	      broadcast_remove_to([ user, "dashboard" ], target: "#{dom_id(self)}-archived")
123	    elsif status == "archived" && prev_status != "archived"
124	      # Active → archived: move the row between lists live. Prepend so the
125	      # most recently archived sandbox appears at the top of the list —
126	      # matches the `archived_at: :desc` order on the dashboard.
127	      broadcast_remove_to([ user, "dashboard" ], target: dom_id(self))
128	      broadcast_prepend_to(
129	        [ user, "dashboard" ],
130	        partial: "dashboard/archived_sandbox",
131	        locals: { sandbox: self },
132	        target: "archived-sandboxes"
133	      )
134	    elsif prev_status == "archived" && !status.in?(%w[destroyed archived])
135	      # Archived → active (restore): move back to active list.
136	      broadcast_remove_to([ user, "dashboard" ], target: "#{dom_id(self)}-archived")
137	      broadcast_prepend_to(
138	        [ user, "dashboard" ],
139	        partial: "dashboard/sandbox",
140	        locals: { sandbox: self },
141	        target: "sandboxes"
142	      )
143	    else
144	      broadcast_replace_to(
145	        [ user, "dashboard" ],
146	        partial: "dashboard/sandbox",
147	        locals: { sandbox: self },
148	        target: dom_id(self)
149	      )
150	    end
151	  rescue => e
152	    Rails.logger.warn("Sandbox#broadcast_replace: #{e.message}")
153	  end
154	
155	  def broadcast_remove_from_dashboard
156	    broadcast_remove_to([ user, "dashboard" ], target: dom_id(self))
157	    broadcast_remove_to([ user, "dashboard" ], target: "#{dom_id(self)}-archived")
158	  rescue => e
159	    Rails.logger.warn("Sandbox#broadcast_remove: #{e.message}")
160	  end
161	end
162

56	    container = Docker::Container.create(
57	      "name" => sandbox.full_name,
58	      "Image" => sandbox.image,
59	      "Hostname" => sandbox.full_name,

68	      }.compact,

70	        "EndpointsConfig" => { NETWORK_NAME => {} }

74	    container.start
75	    container.refresh!

77	      state_error = container.json.dig("State", "Error").presence || container.json.dig("State", "Status")
78	      container.stop rescue nil
79	      container.delete(force: true) rescue nil
80	      raise Error, "Container failed to start: #{state_error}"
81	    end
82	    sandbox.update!(container_id: container.id, status: "running")
83	
84	    # Connect to per-user network for tenant isolation
85	    begin

87	    rescue NetworkManager::Error => e
88	      container.stop rescue nil
89	      container.delete(force: true) rescue nil
90	      sandbox.update!(container_id: nil, status: "pending")
91	      raise Error, "Failed to attach tenant network: #{e.message}"
92	    end
93	
94	    # Pre-write Traefik routes so they're active immediately (no wait on first open).
95	    # Non-critical: routes will be written on first terminal/VNC open if this fails.

98	      VncManager.new.prepare_traefik_config(sandbox) if sandbox.vnc_enabled?

101	    end
102

> AGENT

Root cause: user's template is `ssh://{user}@{tailscale_ip}` — when a fresh sandbox's Tailscale IP isn't yet known (nil from `tailscale_ips_for`), the href becomes `ssh://thies@`, which is not a parseable URL, so Turbo's click handler throws.

Fix: make `expand_custom_link_url` return `nil` when any referenced variable resolves to blank, and the view skips rendering in that case.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01XNv1xyh3DVVLDxtUiXFzNr
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/persisted/Projects/Sandcastle/app/models/user.rb",
  "old_string": "  def expand_custom_link_url(url_template, sandbox:, tailscale_ip: nil)\n    hostname = ENV.fetch(\"SANDCASTLE_HOST\", \"localhost\")\n    url_template.gsub(/\\{(\\w+)\\}/) do |match|\n      case $1\n      when \"user\" then name\n      when \"hostname\" then hostname\n      when \"ssh_port\" then sandbox.ssh_port.to_s\n      when \"sandbox\" then sandbox.name\n      when \"tailscale_ip\" then tailscale_ip.to_s\n      when \"tmux_cmd\" then TMUX_CMD\n      when \"tmux_cmd_encoded\" then ERB::Util.url_encode(TMUX_CMD)\n      else match\n      end\n    end\n  end",
  "new_string": "  # Expands {vars} in a custom-link URL template. Returns nil if any\n  # referenced variable resolves to a blank value (e.g. a fresh sandbox\n  # whose tailscale_ip isn't assigned yet) — callers skip rendering in\n  # that case so Turbo's click handler doesn't choke on an invalid URL.\n  def expand_custom_link_url(url_template, sandbox:, tailscale_ip: nil)\n    hostname = ENV.fetch(\"SANDCASTLE_HOST\", \"localhost\")\n    missing = false\n    expanded = url_template.gsub(/\\{(\\w+)\\}/) do\n      value = case $1\n              when \"user\" then name\n              when \"hostname\" then hostname\n              when \"ssh_port\" then sandbox.ssh_port\n              when \"sandbox\" then sandbox.name\n              when \"tailscale_ip\" then tailscale_ip\n              when \"tmux_cmd\" then TMUX_CMD\n              when \"tmux_cmd_encoded\" then ERB::Util.url_encode(TMUX_CMD)\n              end\n      if value.to_s.empty?\n        missing = true\n        \"\"\n      else\n        value.to_s\n      end\n    end\n    missing ? nil : expanded\n  end"
}

{
  "command": "git add app/services/sandbox_manager.rb && git commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nfeat(sandbox): always recreate container on start\n\nPreviously, start preferred `docker restart` of the existing container,\nwhich preserved the env vars from original creation — so newly added SSH\nkeys and edited sandbox settings only took effect after a full rebuild.\nNow start always destroys and recreates so every start picks up the\nlatest SSH keys (via SANDCASTLE_SSH_KEY env var from user.all_ssh_keys_text)\nand the current per-sandbox settings.\n\nThe Sysbox UID offset changes on recreation, but entrypoint.sh already\nchowns /home/\\$USERNAME and /persisted on every start, so bind-mounted\nfile ownership is remapped automatically.\n\nThe now-unused try_restart_existing helper is removed.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Commit start-always-recreate change"
}

Turbo's click handler calls `new URL(href)` on every click\n(before honoring data-turbo=\"false\"), and an empty host throws\n`Failed to construct 'URL': Invalid URL`, so the link silently didn't\nwork.\n\nexpand_custom_link_url now returns nil when any referenced template\nvariable resolves to a blank value (nil or empty string), and the\ndashboard skips rendering the anchor in that case.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\n)\"",

> AGENT

Pushed:

- `045c952` feat(sandbox): always recreate container on start — so fresh SSH keys and current settings take effect every start (not just rebuild).
- `c6632ca` fix(dashboard): skip custom links whose template vars are unresolved — fixes the Turbo `Failed to construct 'URL'` crash on fresh sandboxes where `{tailscale_ip}` isn't known yet.

Let me know when you want the next release tagged.
